\matheur{M}
(resp.
M
) as an object in
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
(resp.
\mathcal{R}
),
let
\mathcal{T}{M}
(resp.
\mathcal{T}_{M}
) be the strictly full Tannakian subcategory of
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
(resp.
\mathcal{R}
) generated by
\matheur{M}
(resp.
M
).
By Tannakian duality, we have an affine algebraic group scheme
\Gamma{M}
defined over
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
so that
\mathcal{T}{M}
is equivalent to the category
\mathbf{Rep}(\Gamma{M})
of the
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
-finite dimensional representations of the algebraic group
\Gamma{M}
.
We call
\Gamma{M}
the
t
-motivic Galois group of
\matheur{M}
.
The fully faithful functor from
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
to
\mathcal{R}
gives an equivalence between
\mathcal{T}{M}
and
\mathcal{T}_{M}
, whence
\Gamma{M}
is isomorphic to
\Gamma_{M}
defined in Section
2.5
.

Remark 7.1.3
.

We recall the functorial property of the base change
\Gamma_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\mathbb{C}_{\infty}\times_{\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)}\Gamma_{\matheur{M}}
(via the embedding
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)\hookrightarrow\mathbb{C}_{\infty}
by sending
t
to
\theta
) as follows.
Let
\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}
be the restriction of the fiber functor
\boldsymbol{\omega}
to
\mathcal{T}{M}
.
For any object
\mathcal{X}\in\mathcal{T}{M}
we let
A_{\mathcal{X}}\subset\Hom(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M}}(\mathcal{X}),\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M}}(\mathcal{X}))
be defined as in
[
DMOS82
, p. 149]
and
\underline{A}_{\mathcal{X}}\in\mathcal{T}{M}
be defined as in
[
DMOS82
, Remark 3.3]
,
from which
\underline{A}_{\mathcal{X}}
is a ring in
\mathcal{T}{M}
such that
\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}(\underline{A}_{\mathcal{X}})=A_{\mathcal{X}}
as
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
-algebras.
Define

\underline{B}\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\varinjlim_{\mathcal{X}}\underline{A}_{\mathcal{X}}^{\vee}\quad\text{ and }\quad B\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\varinjlim_{\mathcal{X}}A_{\mathcal{X}}^{\vee}.

Then by
[
DMOS82
, Remark 3.3]
we have that

\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}(\underline{B})=B,\quad\underline{\End}^{\otimes}(\boldsymbol{\omega}{M})=\Spec(\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}(\underline{B}))=\Gamma_{\matheur{M}},

and for any fiber functor
\eta:\mathcal{T}_{\matheur{M}}\rightarrow{\mathbf{Proj}_{R}}
where
R
is an
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
-algebra and
{\mathbf{Proj}_{R}}
is the category of finitely generated projective
R
-modules,
the following equality holds:

\underline{\End}^{\otimes}(\eta)=\Spec(\eta(\underline{B})).

In particular, let
R=\mathbb{C}_{\infty}
(i.e.,
\mathbf{Proj}_{R}=\mathbf{Vect}_{/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
) and take
\eta\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\omega_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
, the base change of
\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}
from
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
to
\mathbb{C}_{\infty}
, i.e.,

\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}(\mathcal{X})\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\mathbb{C}_{\infty}\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)}\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}(\mathcal{X}).

Then
\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
is still a tensor functor, and we have that

\displaystyle\underline{\mathrm{Aut}}^{\otimes}(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})=\underline{\End}^{\otimes}(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})

\displaystyle=\Spec(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}(\underline{B}))

\displaystyle=\Spec(\mathbb{C}_{\infty}\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)}\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}(\underline{B}))=\Gamma_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}},

where the first identity comes from
[
DMOS82
, Prop. 1.13]
.

7.2.
Hodge-Pink filtration

Let
\matheur{M}
be an object in
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
.
Recall that
H_{\mathrm{Betti}}(\matheur{M})
actually lies in
\mathbb{M}^{\dagger}
, and the natural map

\mathbb{T}^{\dagger}\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_{q}[t]}H_{\mathrm{Betti}}(\matheur{M})\longrightarrow\mathbb{M}^{\dagger}

is an isomorphism as
\mathbb{T}^{\dagger}
-modules.
Put

\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]\otimes_{\bar{k}[t]}\matheur{M}\quad\text{ and }\quad H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}(\matheur{M})=\mathbb{C}_{\infty}\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)}H{M}.

The above isomorphism induces the chain of isomorphisms

(7.2.1)

H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\cong\mathbb{C}_{\infty}\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_{q}[t]}H_{\mathrm{Betti}}(\matheur{M})\cong\frac{\mathbb{T}^{\dagger}}{(t-\theta)\mathbb{T}^{\dagger}}\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_{q}[t]}H_{\mathrm{Betti}}(\matheur{M})\\
\cong\frac{\mathbb{T}^{\dagger}}{(t-\theta)\mathbb{T}^{\dagger}}\otimes_{\bar{k}[t]}\matheur{M}\cong\frac{\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}{(t-\theta)\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}.

Definition 7.2.2
.

(cf.
[
HaJu20
, Section 2.4.5]
) For each object
\matheur{M}
in the category
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
,
the
Hodge-Pink filtration of
\matheur{M}
is
F^{\bullet}H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=(F^{i}H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})_{i\in\mathbb{Z}}
, where
F^{i}H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
is the image of
\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\cap(t-\theta)^{i}(\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})
in
H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
, through the identification (
7.2.1
) for each
i\in\mathbb{Z}
. That is,

H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\supseteq F^{i}H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\cong\frac{\bigl(\matheur{M}\cap(t-\theta)^{i}(\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})\bigr)+(t-\theta)\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}{(t-\theta)\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}.

Remark 7.2.3
.

Given an object
\matheur{M}
of
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
, there exists a sufficiently large integer
n
such that

(7.2.4)

(t-\theta)^{n}\matheur{M}\subseteq\sigma\matheur{M}\subseteq(t-\theta)^{-n}\matheur{M}.

Let
r
be the rank of
\matheur{M}
over
\bar{k}[t]
.
There exists a unique (non-ordered)
r
-tuple of integers
(w_{1},\ldots,w_{r})
so that

\frac{(t-\theta)^{-n}\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}{\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}\cong\bigoplus_{i=1}^{r}\frac{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]}{(t-\theta)^{n-w_{i}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]}.

We call
(w_{1},\dots,w_{r})
the
Hodge-Pink weights of
\matheur{M}
.
In other words,
w_{1},\dots,w_{r}
are the “jumps” of the Hodge-Pink filtration of
\matheur{M}
.
In particular,
\matheur{M}
is effective if and only if
w_{1},\dots,w_{r}
are all non-positive.

We fix an object
\matheur{M}\in\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
and let
\{m_{1},\ldots,m_{r}\}
be a basis of the free
\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]
-module
\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
so that

\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}=\bigoplus_{i=1}^{r}(t-\theta)^{-w_{i}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]\cdot m_{i}\quad\subset\mathbb{C}_{\infty}(t)\otimes_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]}\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}.

Identifying
H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
with
\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}/(t-\theta)\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
via (
7.2.1
), let
v_{1},\ldots,v_{r}\in H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
be the images of
m_{1},\dots,m_{r}
through the identification (
7.2.1
).
It is straightforward to see that

F^{i_{0}}H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}=\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq r\\
w_{i}\geq i_{0}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}v_{i}\quad\forall\,i_{0}\in\mathbb{Z}.

In particular, we have the associated graded spaces

(7.2.5)

\mathrm{Gr}^{i_{0}}(H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i\leq r\\
w_{i}=i_{0}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}v_{i}\cong\frac{F^{i_{0}}H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}{F^{i_{0}+1}H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}},\quad\forall\,i_{0}\in\mathbb{Z},

and

(7.2.6)

H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}=\bigoplus_{i_{0}\in\mathbb{Z}}{\rm Gr}^{i_{0}}(H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}).

Lemma 7.2.7
.

Given two objects
\matheur{M}
and
\matheur{M}^{\prime}
in
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
, we
let
\matheur{M}_{0}\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}=\matheur{M}\otimes_{\bar{k}[t]}\matheur{M}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
, on which
\sigma
acts diagonally. The natural isomorphism
H_{\matheur{M}_{0},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\cong H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}H_{\matheur{M}^{\prime},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}

induces

\Gr^{i_{0}}(H_{\matheur{M}_{0},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})\cong\bigoplus_{j_{0}+j_{0}^{\prime}=i_{0}}\mathrm{Gr}^{j_{0}}(H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})\otimes_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\mathrm{Gr}^{j_{0}^{\prime}}(H_{\matheur{M}^{\prime},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}),\quad\forall i_{0}\in\mathbb{Z}.

Proof.

Let
(w_{1},\ldots,w_{r})
(resp.
(w_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,w_{r}^{\prime})
) be the Hodge-Pink weights of
\matheur{M}
(resp.
\matheur{M}^{\prime}
).
Take
\{m_{1},\ldots,m_{r}\}
(resp.
\{m_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,m_{r}^{\prime}\}
) to be a basis of the free
\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]
-module
\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
(resp.
\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}^{\prime}
) so that

\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}=\bigoplus_{j=1}^{r}(t-\theta)^{-w_{j}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]\cdot m_{j}\quad\text{ and }\quad\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}^{\prime}=\bigoplus_{j^{\prime}=1}^{r}(t-\theta)^{-w_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]\cdot m_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}.

Then
\{m_{j}\otimes m^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}}:1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq r\}
is a basis of
\matheur{M}_{0,\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
so that

\sigma\matheur{M}_{0,\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}=\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]}\sigma\matheur{M}_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}^{\prime}=\bigoplus_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq r}(t-\theta)^{-(w_{j}+w_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime})}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}[t]\cdot m_{j}\otimes m^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}}.

This means that
w_{j}+w_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}
, for
1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq r
, are the Hodge-Pink weights of
\matheur{M}_{0}
.

For
1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq r
, let
v_{j}
,
v^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}}
, and
v_{j,j^{\prime}}
be the image of
m_{j}
,
m^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}}
, and
m_{j}\otimes m^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}}
in
H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
,
H_{\matheur{M}^{\prime},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
, and
H_{\matheur{M}_{0},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
, respectively (through the identification (
7.2.1
)).
The canonical isomorphism
H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\otimes_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}H_{\matheur{M}^{\prime},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\cong H_{\matheur{M}_{0},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}

sending
v_{j}\otimes v^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}}
to
v_{j,j^{\prime}}
and the isomorphism in (
7.2.5
) implies that for each
i_{0}\in\mathbb{Z}
,

\displaystyle{\Gr}^{i_{0}}(H_{\matheur{M}_{0},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})

\displaystyle\cong\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq r\\
w_{j}+w_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}=i_{0}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}v_{j,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{j_{0}+j_{0}^{\prime}=i_{0}}\Biggl(\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq r\\
w_{j}=j_{0},\ w_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}=j_{0}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}v_{j,j^{\prime}}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\cong\bigoplus_{j_{0}+j_{0}^{\prime}=i_{0}}\Biggl[\biggl(\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j\leq r\\
w_{j}=j_{0}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}v_{j}\biggr)\otimes_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\biggl(\bigoplus_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j^{\prime}\leq r\\
w_{j^{\prime}}^{\prime}=j_{0}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{C}_{\infty}v^{\prime}_{j^{\prime}}\biggr)\Biggr]

\displaystyle\cong\bigoplus_{j_{0}+j_{0}^{\prime}=i_{0}}{\Gr}^{j_{0}}(H_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})\otimes_{\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}{\Gr}^{j_{0}^{\prime}}(H_{\matheur{M}^{\prime},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}).

∎

In the next subsection we shall use the Hodge-Pink filtration of the given object
\matheur{M}
in
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
to construct a special family of cocharacters of the
t
-motivic Galois group
\Gamma{M}
.

7.3.
Hodge-Pink cocharacters

Given an object
\matheur{M}
in
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
,
let
\mathcal{T}_{M}
be the neutral Tannikian subcategory of
\mathcal{T}
over
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
generated by the pre-
t
-motive
M=\bar{k}(t)\otimes_{\bar{k}[t]}\matheur{M}
(see Definition
2.5.2
(2)).
Recall in
7.1
that the Tannakian subcategory
\mathcal{T}{M}
of
\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
generated by
\matheur{M}
is equivalent to
\mathcal{T}_{M}
, and so we have the algebraic group isomorphism (over
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
)

\Gamma{M}\cong\Gamma_{M}.

Note that we have a faithful representation

\Gamma_{M}\hookrightarrow\GL(H_{\matheur{M}}),

which is functorial in
M
(see
[
P08
, Thm. 4.5.3]
). This implies that

(7.3.1)

\Gamma_{M}\hookrightarrow{\Cent}_{\GL(H_{\matheur{M}})}\left(\End_{\mathcal{T}}(M)\right)\quad\textup{(cf.~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{CPY10}{}{}, p.~1448, (6)]})}

as we have the natural embedding
\End_{\mathcal{T}}(M)\hookrightarrow\End(H_{\matheur{M}})
.

We let
\mathbb{G}_{m/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
be the multiplicative group over
\mathbb{C}_{\infty}
, and we denote by
\mathbf{Rep}(\mathbb{G}_{m/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})
the Tannakian category of finite dimensional algebraic representations of
\mathbb{G}_{m/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
over
\mathbb{C}_{\infty}
. Note that by
[
DMOS82
, p. 144, (2.30)]
,
\mathbf{Rep}(\mathbb{G}_{m/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})
is equivalent to the category
{\mathbf{Grad.Vect}}_{/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
of
\mathbb{Z}
-graded vector spaces of finite dimensions over
\mathbb{C}_{\infty}
.
The fiber functor, which is also the forgetful functor, is denoted by

\boldsymbol{\omega}^{\mathbb{G}_{m/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}}:\mathbf{Rep}(\mathbb{G}_{m/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}})\rightarrow{\mathbf{Vect}}_{/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}.

For any object
\matheur{M}\in\mathcal{H}\mathcal{J}^{I}_{U}
, we have the following functor (induced by the Hodge-Pink filtrations),

\mathcal{F}:\mathcal{T}{M}\rightarrow{\mathbf{Grad.Vect}}_{/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}},\quad\mathcal{F}(\matheur{M}^{\prime})\mathrel{\vbox{\hbox{\scriptsize.}\hbox{\scriptsize.}}}={\oplus_{i\in\mathbb{Z}}\rm{Gr}}^{i}H_{M^{\prime},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}.

By Lemma
7.2.7
, one checks that
\mathcal{F}
is a tensor functor.
On the other hand, recall from Remark
7.1.3
that
\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
denotes the base change of the fiber functor
\boldsymbol{\omega}{M}
from
\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)
to
\mathbb{C}_{\infty}
, i.e.

\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}:\mathcal{T}{M}\rightarrow\mathbf{Vect}_{/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}},\quad\boldsymbol{\omega}_{\matheur{M},\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}(\matheur{M}^{\prime})=\mathbb{C}_{\infty}\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_{q}(t)}H_{\matheur{M}^{\prime}}.

Let
\boldsymbol{\omega}^{\rm GV}:\mathbf{Grad.Vect}_{/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}\rightarrow\mathbf{Vect}_{/\mathbb{C}_{\infty}}
be the forgetful functor.
By (
7.2.6
) we then have the following commutative diagram

\lx@xy@svg{\hbox{\raise 2.5pt\hbox{\kern 25.9479pt\hbox{\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\hbox{\vtop{\halign{\entry@#!@&&\entry@@#!@\cr&&&\cr&&\crcr}}}\ignorespaces{\hbox{\kern-25.9479pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{({\mathbf{Rep}}(\Gamma_{\matheur{M}})}$}}}}}}}{\hbox{\kern 61.8869pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\raise-2.5pt\hbox{$\textstyle{\hskip-25.6073pt)\hskip 93.51936pt\raise 5.39168pt\hbox{{}\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 3.0pt\hbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\raise-2.39168pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathcal{F}}$}}}\kern
